                  (B1a's screen: a fragment the speaker said in that language is legitimate).
  wrongAny      = the same screen over every output, refused or not (B1c for English).
  withList      = outputs carrying a list line (B3 reads it on prose fixtures only).


════ PER FIXTURE, per arm (never pooled)
